near ends cheap, far ones dear, so circuits that work together gather in one part of tag space near the ports they use], populations competing for their destination [ISL_COMPETE=1: the populations into a destination run strongest grip first, ties in genome order, and each word an earlier population's kernels wrote this step is claimed, and a kernel whose output words are mostly claimed is held down and learns anti-Hebbian, so the winners write the act, not the OR of every reflex] and pruning [ISL_PRUNE=1: each population holds its destination with its gene grip, 16 to start, up one a step it writes, down one a step it only loses, letting go for the lifetime at 0]) with branches (ISL_BRANCH=1: a population's fired kernels may also write their outputs into a second vector, a channel or an output port, at the same place by OR, as an axon's collaterals: an efference copy, a line to another population's inhibit input, two targets driven by one decision; a new population has one one time in four; under competition a branch writes only unclaimed words); 600 generations
